				is_in_faction_with = BRG
				exists = no
			}
		}
	}
	x = 0
	y = 1
	completion_reward = {
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Focus HGR_allies_through_thick_and_thin"
		custom_effect_tooltip = HGR_incorporate_KSS_tt
		add_to_variable = { HGR_ss_pragmatist_support_army_attack_factor = 0.15 }
		add_to_variable = { HGR_ss_pragmatist_support_army_defense_factor = 0.15 }
		KSS = {
			increase_Heydrich_influence_huge = yes
			if = {
				limit = {
					KSS = {
						is_neighbor_of = GER
					}
				}
				KSS = {
					every_unit_leader = {
						set_nationality = GER
					}
				}
				GER = {
					annex_country = {
						target = KSS
						transfer_troops = yes
					}
				}
			}
			else = {
				add_to_faction = KSS
			}
		}
	}
}

	}

				}
			}

			}

shared_focus = {
	id = HGR_sort_out_ukraine
	icon = GFX_UKR_Free_Ukraine
	cost = 1
	relative_position_id = HGR_the_return
	prerequisite = {
		focus = HGR_annex_netherlands
	}
	prerequisite = {
		focus = HGR_springtime_for_polska
	}
	prerequisite = {
		focus = HGR_annex_ostland
	}
	bypass = {
		
	}
	x = 0
	y = 2
	completion_reward = {
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Focus HGR_sort_out_ukraine"
		#trigger for balkan troubles
		hidden_effect = {
			HUN = {
				country_event = {
					id = ZZZ_balkan_war.1
					days = 62
				}
			}
		}
		UKR = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_government = national_socialism
				}
				save_party_popularities = yes
				GER = {
					set_autonomy = {
						target = UKR
						autonomous_state = autonomy_autonomous_reichskommissariat
						end_wars = no
						end_civil_wars = no
					}
				}
				apply_party_popularities = yes
				add_ideas = Pakt_Autonomous_Reichskommissariat
				set_politics = {
					ruling_party = national_socialism
				}
			}
			else = {
				save_party_popularities = yes
				GER = {
					set_autonomy = {
						target = UKR
						autonomous_state = autonomy_collaborator_puppet_state
						end_wars = no
						end_civil_wars = no
					}
				}
				apply_party_popularities = yes
				add_ideas = Pakt_Marionettenstaat
				set_politics = {
					ruling_party = authoritarian_democrat
				}
			}
			remove_ideas = UKR_shroud_of_generalplan_ost
		}
	}
}

					is_subject = no
				}
			}

				target = ULS
				type = annex_everything
			}
		}
	}
